    RN-699 — The Dev Lead reads the current module_map and the PROJECT backlog, and rereads both when the plan is refused

    Measured in real use (10/02): with the Dev Lead accepted, he replied that the module_map was missing — and the map existed. The kickoff looked for the architecture.module_map_created event, which NO code emits (the Arquiteto records the map through the api), and always fell into "(sem module_map)". The map now comes from the SAME project read the AppSec uses (get_infra_context, the api's current one), never from a session event; without a map, the kickoff says there is none.

    In the same use, the tasks were born AFTER the kickoff, the Dev Lead invented a task_id, the api refused the plan and the turn ended without a new attempt. He gets read_backlog: a CONTAINED read, without parameters, scoped to the project (the pending tasks through the same route as the PO's listar_backlog, a ceiling of 200 lines stating the real total, and the current module_map). The api's refusal goes back to the loop saying to call read_backlog and fix it; and only a successful propose_execution_plan ends the turn — before, any successful tool in the last call (a read, an emit_artifact) ended it. The kickoff instruction forbids inventing task_id and asks him to only say he assessed the stories on which he called assess_implementability.

    RN-700 — The PO does not announce the handoff to the Arquiteto without doing it, and a story without tasks is not delivered

    In real use the PO wrote "Handoff ao Arquiteto" and ended the turn without calling offer_handoff (violating RN-163), and delivered five ready stories with zero tasks — the Dev Lead had nothing to distribute. At the end of every PO turn, if the server has not yet recorded an offer to the Arquiteto in that process, it reads the project's rules (uncoveredCount, the same coverage as the automatic acceptance of RN-660) and the backlog: with every rule covered and every story with ≥ 1 task, the SERVER offers the handoff (the mold of the Infra that proposes on its own, ADR 0190, RN-671/672), and RN-660 keeps deciding the acceptance. With rules covered and a story without tasks, it does not offer and records backlog.handoff_not_offered (origin modelo) naming the stories; a read or offer that fails records the same event with origin infra. An uncovered rule, or a backlog without rules/stories, is not judged. The PO's instruction now requires a task per story. The one who creates the tasks is the PO (decision of the main agent in AT-364: he already has create_task).

    The installer offers the local models, and does not mistake dev for an installation (RN-691, RN-692)

    RN-702 — Context compaction respects the default 128k window and never swaps turns for an empty marker

    • Rule: without context_window in the context (the conversational agents do not declare it), the compaction window is DEFAULT_CONTEXT_WINDOW, whose default went from 8,192 to 128,000, the same value the gate and dev agents declare. When the summarizer (context-manager) fails or returns empty text, the summary is DETERMINISTIC (an excerpt of up to 300 characters of each replaced turn) and context.compacted records summaryOrigin: "deterministico" and falha with origem and motivo (RN-059). With the summarizer responding, summaryOrigin: "modelo" and falha: null.
    • Measurement (AT-366): session 17d41c20, Arquiteto on anthropic/claude-haiku-4.5 (200k window): 6,799 tokens exceeded 0.7 × 8,192 = 5,734 and compacted 35 turns; the summarizer failed and the recorded summary was the marker "(35 turnos anteriores omitidos)". On the next turn the Arquiteto asked again for a confirmation it had already received.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/context_manager.ex:157 (summarize), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/context_manager.ex:188 (resumo_deterministico), apps/engine/config/runtime.exs (default_context_window)

    • Declared gap: the window is still not read from the per-model catalog (the engine does not receive the binding's context_window); 128k is the installation default, adjustable through DEFAULT_CONTEXT_WINDOW.
    • Origin: AT-366

    RN-706 — The execution agent knows where it is: folder, image, network, typed git and its own module

    • Rule: the dev agents (dev-*) and the QA subagents (qa-*) receive on every LLM call an EPHEMERAL environment system message, through the same path as the language (RN-622) and the author's profile (RN-680) — never in state.messages, never per server. It states the real folder in which the terminal runs (the loop's workspace_root, translated to /work by the same rule as TerminalExecutor when there is a REGISTERED running container), the image and network of the last artifact.project_image, that git is not run in the terminal (use git_commit/git_push/pr_open) and, for the dev agent, ITS MODULE: what it consumes from another module comes from the contract (listar_contratos_de_modulos) and is used through stub/mock, never reimplemented, and root files that already exist on dev (package.json, lockfile) are not recreated. Without a running container, the message SAYS so and does not assert /work. Ceiling of 1,200 characters; a read failure becomes an absent message, never a crashed turn.
    • Measurement (AT-379/AT-378, 10/02, loja-teste): dev-api and qa-automacao ran cd /workspace (no prompt cites that folder); git status gave exit 127 in an image without git; dev-api (module api) created src/catalog.js and package.json, the same as dev-catalog's, and the merge conflicted.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/ambiente_do_agente.ex:102 (montar), :48 (com_ambiente), :65 (anexar), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tool_loop.ex:87 (com_ambiente)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/ambiente_do_agente_test.exs ("caminho feliz: com container running, a pasta é /work/.worktrees/"; failure case: "sem container running, não afirma /work")
    • Declared gap: no notice in the git_commit result for a file of another module (the module_map does not say which paths belong to each module, so the notice would not be deterministic) and no commit block, by owner decision.
    • Origin: AT-379, AT-378

    RN-707 — The SecOps gate's semgrep has a writable $HOME, and the output-less failure is named

    • Rule: in the production and installation composes, the engine's tmpfs /home/engine is born owned by the image's user (/home/engine:uid=1000,gid=1000,mode=0700). SemgrepDetector.Live distinguishes exit 1 with JSON (findings) from exit 0/1 with empty stdout, which becomes {:error, {:sem_saida, exit_code}} instead of :invalid_output. The gate's verdict does NOT change: a scanner that did not run is still "skipped" in the verdict's summary.
    • Measurement (AT-380): inside brabo-engine-1 (production image, read_only: true), the tmpfs /home/engine was born root 2755; the gate's semgrep scan exited 1 with empty stdout and PermissionError: '/home/engine/.semgrep' on stderr, and the parser called that :invalid_output. With the tmpfs uid=1000 the same command returns the JSON (exit 0). Kubernetes did not have the defect (fsGroup: 1000 on the emptyDir).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/actions/semgrep_detector.ex:73 (interpretar), docker/docker-compose.prod.yml, docker/docker-compose.install.yml
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/actions/semgrep_detector_test.exs ("JSON com results vira findings, inclusive com exit 1 (achou algo)"; failure case: "exit 1 com stdout vazio é falha de execução nomeada, não :invalid_output")
    • Decision (revised by RN-714): the sentence "the gate's verdict does NOT change" no longer applies: the owner decided (10/02) that SAST that did not run leaves the gate PENDING and blocking.
    • Origin: AT-380

    RN-710 — Workflows is not delegated without a CI that runs, and asks no one

    On the Infra's acceptance, infra-workflows replied with a question ("what is the project repository's provider?") that no human receives — a subagent does not converse —, and the delegation ended failed with origin modelo and a generic dev.error in the thread (measured in loja-teste, 10/02). The project was local: a bare repository on the server, with no CI runner.

    • Provider local or absent: delegation to Workflows is dispensed (delegation dispensed, with the named justification — "repositório local … não tem CI que rode"), and the infra PR goes out with only what the Lead produced. Only github and gitlab delegate.

    • When it delegates, the prompt says that the received provider ALREADY is the project's and forbids asking.

    • A subagent that stops with TEXT instead of calling the tool is still a named failure reported to the lead, but the diagnosis says what it asked for: "pediu informação que não recebeu, em vez de chamar emit_infra_delegation_result: " (up to 300 characters).

    • The infra PR's title carries the infra: prefix only when the model's summary does not already start with it (case-insensitively) — before, it came out "infra: infra: Dockerfile…" (AT-396).

    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/infra/workflows_agent.ex:61 (dispensa), apps/engine/lib/engine/infra/infra_lead.ex:69 (com_prefixo), apps/engine/lib/engine/infra/workflows_agent.ex:184 (parada_sem_ferramenta), apps/engine/lib/engine/infra/infra_lead_server.ex:1217 (finalize)

    RN-711 — The conversational agent knows where it is in the flow, and the user's technical restriction becomes a decision_record

    • Rule: the seven conversational agents (Criativo, PO, Arquiteto, Infra, Dev Lead, UX Designer, Staff) receive on every LLM call an EPHEMERAL system message with the delivery flow — the seven stages in order (Criativo → PO → Arquiteto → Infra → Dev Lead → dev agents → QA/SecOps → manual merge), WHERE the agent is, the real NEXT step and WHO triggers it (human × system) —, through the EngineApiClient facade, after the environment (RN-706) and before the profile (RN-680) and the language (RN-622, always the last). The source is ONE, Engine.Harness.FluxoDoTime, checked by a test against docs/fluxo.yml: every role cited exists there as active, and the text does not cite a stage outside it (deploy, pipeline, Dockerfile, InfraAgent). The Criativo records the user's EXPLICIT technical restriction (language, "no dependency", "no network", "a single module") as a decision_record (emit_artifact, RN-505), without a lecture; the Arquiteto's kickoff reads the session's artifact.decision_record (the same read by type, RN-580) and says to respect them, stating WHICH and WHY when going against one. The Arquiteto's wrap-up does not ask again for a confirmation already given, and the Infra does not paste the files into the chat nor promise deploy.

    • Measurement (AT-369/AT-370, 10/02, loja-teste): the Criativo announced the Arquiteto as next (it is the PO); the PO said the Arquiteto would "implement"; the Arquiteto, that "dev agents start" after the ADRs, and asked "Confirme: Arquitetura pronta" after the confirmation; the Infra dumped ~80 lines of compose into the thread. The request "Node puro, nenhuma dependência, sem rede, um módulo só" did not become an artifact and the Arquiteto created three modules.

    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/fluxo_do_time.ex:107 (texto), :97 (anexar), apps/engine/lib/engine/sessions/engine_api_client.ex:647

      :97 (anexar), apps/engine/lib/engine/sessions/engine_api_client.ex:700 (llm_turn), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/agents.ex:23 (identity), apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/arquiteto_server.ex:388 (build_kickoff)

    • Declared gap: the Arquiteto's decision_record read is the SESSION's (like the kickoff's rules and stories), not the whole project's; and nothing REFUSES a create_module_map that goes against the restriction — the instruction asks for the justification, it does not impose it.

    RN-714 — SAST that did not run leaves the SecOps gate pending, never approved

    • Rule: when the SecOps gate's semgrep does not run — missing binary, invalid output (:invalid_output), :sem_saida, exception or time ceiling —, no verdict is recorded: the PR stays in awaiting_secops, the gate stays PENDING and blocking, and the PR does not reach the user as approved by SecOps. The reason goes to the thread as an agent.error of origin infra ("SAST não rodou: — … reexecute o gate depois de corrigir"). The external contract does not change: the usual two verdicts remain (approved/changes_requested), one per gate. A missing gitleaks is still only "skipped" in the summary.
    • Re-run: the cycle stays in_progress in gate_states, and the GateRescuer re-runs it at engine boot and after the staleness threshold (gate_rescue_stale_after_seconds, 15 min). There is no re-run gate button on screen.
    • No repetition (AT-386): a rescue that finds the SAME reason again does not record another agent.error: if the SecOps's last event for that task (agent.error or artifact.secops_verdict, read by type, tail with a ceiling) is already this error with the same reason, only the server's Logger line remains. A different reason, a verdict in between or a failing read record again; the agent.error now carries taskId.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/secops_agent_server.ex:116 (run_secops), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/secops_agent_server.ex:144 (sast_nao_rodou), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/secops_agent_server.ex:194 (erro_ja_registrado?)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/secops_agent_server_test.exs ("sem achados (gitleaks e semgrep limpos): approved"; failure cases: "semgrep com saída inválida: gate pendente, sem veredito, motivo nomeado" and "semgrep com binário ausente: gate pendente, sem veredito, motivo nomeado"; AT-386: "primeiro resgate grava; o segundo, com o mesmo motivo, não", "motivo diferente, ou veredito depois do erro, grava de novo", "o SAST que volta a rodar aprova como sempre")
    • Revises: RN-707 ("o veredito do gate NÃO muda")
    • Origin: AT-380

    RN-717 — The agent deviation the Anamnese observes becomes a product signal, without personal data

    • Rule: an agent loop, a turn cancelled by the user and a user message that repairs an agent failure are recorded by the Anamnese as anamnese.agent_deviation (tool report_agent_deviation), never as the person's profile. The payload is assembled in the engine by an allowlist — agente, tipo (laco | cancelamento_pelo_usuario | reparo_pelo_usuario) and evidenceEventIds (deduplicated, ceiling of 20) —, without free text and without the user's id, name, e-mail or trait; an extra field is discarded. The type is not projected into the user's graph. A type outside the vocabulary or without evidence is refused, without recording.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/anamnese/tools/report_agent_deviation.ex:73 (payload), apps/engine/lib/engine/anamnese/tools/report_agent_deviation.ex:52 (run), apps/engine/lib/engine/anamnese/tools.ex, rule in the kickoff (prompts/anamnese-kickoff.md v4)

    One cache key, one question (RN-718)

    RN-719 — An agent tool accepts a list sent as JSON text, and an exception inside it goes back to the loop

    Context: measured live (10/02, Haiku 4.5 via OpenRouter), the model sends lists and objects as a JSON STRING ("business_rule_ids": "[\"01M…\"]"). Three outcomes: create_story silently discarded the ids (the story stayed draft), other tools refused and cost rounds, and create_c4_diagram did Enum.map on a string and brought down the Arquiteto's turn with agent.error.

    The rule: every tool of the seven conversational agents and of the ToolLoop runs through ONE point, Engine.Harness.ArgumentosDeFerramenta.executar/4. An argument whose schema declares array/object and arrives as a valid JSON string of the type becomes the value; a string that is not is a NAMED refusal ("o campo X precisa ser uma lista"), before the tool runs. A field declared string is never touched. An exception raised inside the tool becomes tool.result ok: false with origem: "codigo", and the error enters the loop like any other (RN-163): the turn continues.

    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/argumentos_de_ferramenta.ex:26 (executar), :73 (coagir), :52 (falha_interna?); apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/resultado_de_ferramenta.ex:29 (payload); apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tool_loop.ex:348 (run_direct); apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/arquiteto_server.ex:331 (executar_ferramenta, the same helper in the other six servers)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/argumentos_de_ferramenta_test.exs (create_story with ids in a JSON string records the ids; create_c4_diagram with actors in a JSON string works; a non-JSON string in an array field is a named refusal; a tool that throws does not end the ToolLoop turn and records tool.result ok: false)
    • Origin: AT-387

    RN-721 — A shortened story or task id is resolved within the project

    The Arquiteto passed to assign_story_modules the first 8 characters of the story's id, the api responded 400 "storyId must be a UUID" and, after eight attempts, no story got a module (loja-teste-2, 10/02). Every agent tool that receives a story or task id — assign_story_modules and create_task (story_id), assess_implementability (storyId) and propose_execution_plan (tarefas[].taskId) — passes the id through Engine.Harness.IdDoBacklog before calling the api:

    • A full UUID goes on without a query.

    • A prefix of at least 8 hexadecimal characters is resolved against the PROJECT backlog (list_backlog): a single id starting with it becomes the UUID; none is a named refusal ("nenhuma história do projeto tem id começando com …"); more than one is a named refusal ("o prefixo … é ambíguo"). The refusal goes back to the model as the tool result (RN-163).

    • What is neither a UUID nor a prefix goes on as it came, and the api refuses it.

    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/id_do_backlog.ex:21 (resolver), :43 (resolver_em); apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/dev_lead_tools.ex:176 (resolver_tarefas)

    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/id_do_backlog_test.exs (a unique prefix resolves; ambiguous and nonexistent refuse by name)

    • Origin: AT-390

    The Anamnese does not pay twice for the same window, and gate rescue does not restart a cycle that waits for someone (RN-722)

    RN-722 — The Anamnese closes the window it evaluated and only runs with new interaction; gate rescue asks about the task, respects the pending human decision and parks the old cycle

    • Rule (Anamnese): (1) a round that EVALUATED the window and ended without a profile (skip_proficiency) records anamnese_runs with zero profiles, and the next one starts at window_to — revising the clause of RN-680/the moduledoc "a round that does not conclude does not record": a round that FAILS (ceiling, budget, provider) still does not record, and one that never reaches the LLM (no subject, triage, daily ceiling) closes nothing; (2) the events the Anamnese itself writes (anamnese.* and every event of actor agent/anamnese) and those of actor system stay out of the window and of the triage count; (3) owner decision of 10/02: a subject is whoever has at least 5 NEW own interactions (events of actor_kind: "user" plus decisions, in the window that already starts at the end of the last attempt) — revising the "at least ONE" of RN-680 —, and the project has at most 4 paid rounds per UTC day (concluded, failed or run_skipped written by the model; the no-subject skip does not count). Below either of the two, no LLM or RAG call; the reason goes to the log and, only in a manually requested round, to anamnese.run_skipped (causa: "teto_diario" or "sem_sujeito_elegivel").
    • Rule (gate rescue, revises RN-140): the GateRescuer (a) considers alive the cycle whose TASK is in flight in the lead, by asking it ({:em_voo?, task_id}), and no longer the project's process — an idle lead does not hold the rescue; a lead too busy to answer counts as alive; (b) does not restart a cycle whose session has a PENDING proposed_action from a gate actor (qa*, secops*, appsec) — it only logs; (c) owner decision of 10/02: a cycle stalled for more than 2 h since its LAST ACTIVITY (updated_at) is PARKED (parked_at) with gate.rescue_parked in the session log (task, gate, age, reason), is resumed neither at boot nor on the tick, and resuming requires a human gesture (GateRescuer.retomar_estacionado/3).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/workers/anamnese_worker.ex:189 (fechar_janela), apps/engine/lib/engine/workers/anamnese_worker.ex:104 (dentro_do_teto_diario?), apps/engine/lib/engine/session_events/event.ex:194 (project_window_query), apps/engine/lib/engine/session_events/event.ex:202 (count_anamnese_rounds_since), apps/engine/lib/engine/anamnese/elegibilidade.ex:67 (min_interacoes_proprias), apps/api/src/application/use-cases/anamnese/record-proficiency.use-case.ts:160 (fecharJanelaSemPerfil), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/gate_rescuer.ex:336 (task_em_voo?), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/gate_rescuer.ex:360 (aguardando_humano?), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/gate_rescuer.ex:283 (estacionar), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/gate_rescuer.ex:320 (retomar_estacionado), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_lead_server.ex:95 (handle_call)

    RN-723 — The Infra inherits the network the Arquiteto declared for the project

    Owner decision on 10/02 (ADR 0208), revising the literal none network of the start on acceptance of RN-671. The Infra's election uses the network of the most recent artifact.project_image emitted by the Arquiteto (actor_id: "arquiteto", any session of the project), read locally by the engine; without a decision of his, or with a value outside none/egress, it stays none. It applies to the server's start on acceptance and to the propose_container_start tool without network. The seeded autonomy does not change: egress starts without a click, and the price (the egress network reaches the machine's published ports, ADR 0189) is declared in the ADR.

    • Code: apps/engine/lib/engine/session_events/event.ex:139 (rede_do_arquiteto), apps/engine/lib/engine/infra/infra_lead_server.ex:740 (rede_herdada)
    • Tests: apps/engine/test/engine/infra/infra_lead_server_test.exs — "RN-723: a subida herda a rede egress que o Arquiteto declarou" and "RN-723: rede do Arquiteto fora do vocabulário — a subida segue none"
    • Origin: AT-392

    The Anamnese does not pay twice for the same window, and gate rescue does not restart a cycle that waits for someone (RN-722)

    RN-724 — The parked gate cycle comes back through a button in the thread, not only through the operator's terminal

    • Rule: the gate cycle PARKED by the rescue (RN-722, ADR 0207) is resumed through POST /projects/:projectId/tasks/:taskId/gates/:gate/resume, with the SAME minimum role as whoever decides the PR (developer). The api only authorizes and asks the engine (POST /internal/projects/:id/tasks/:taskId/gates/:gate/resume), which delegates to GateRescuer.retomar_estacionado/3: a cycle that does not exist, is not parked or a gate outside qa/secops is 409 gate_nao_estacionado and nothing is dispatched. In the session thread, the gate.rescue_parked event becomes a notice with the "Retomar gate" button, inert with the reason in text when the role does not reach developer; the refusal shows the api's sentence. No new poll: the resumed gate's progress arrives through the session log.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine_web/controllers/gate_command_controller.ex:15 (resume), apps/api/src/application/use-cases/gates/resume-parked-gate.use-case.ts:18 (execute), apps/api/src/infrastructure/http-clients/api-to-engine-client.ts:351 (resumeParkedGate), apps/api/src/interfaces/http/gates/gate-resume.controller.ts:40 (resume), apps/web/src/routes/RetomarGateEstacionado.tsx:59 (RetomarGateEstacionado)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine_web/controllers/gate_command_controller_test.exs ("ciclo estacionado: 202 e despacha o gate de novo"; failure: "ciclo NÃO estacionado: 409 nomeado e nada despachado"), apps/api/test/interfaces/http/gates/gate-resume.controller.spec.ts (happy, 409 gate_nao_estacionado, role below developer), apps/web/src/routes/RetomarGateEstacionado.test.tsx (the click calls the route; the refusal becomes a toast with the api's sentence; an insufficient role leaves the button inert)
    • Origin: AT-397, closing the gap declared in RN-722

    RN-725 — Text with a literal escape is decoded, and the argument refusal says what arrived

    Context: in TP-01 (10/03, Claude Haiku 4.5) the PO recorded story titles with the LITERAL unicode escape (código): the normalization of RN-719 only decoded array/object fields. Recreated with the right title, the stories went through the duplicate refusal (RN-720/RN-081), which did not match the escaped one with the normal one. And the "exige X" refusals did not say what had arrived: the PO spent 28+ steps (48% of the test's cost) up to the ceiling, and complete_story replied "ainda faltam RF, DoD, DoR ou business_rule_ids" without saying which.

    The rule:

    1. At the same point as RN-719, a string with a LITERAL \uXXXX (surrogate pair included) is decoded, and with it \n, \t, \" and \\ of the SAME string. A string without \uXXXX comes back byte for byte — code in a content with a literal "\n" does not change. This revises RN-719's "a string field is never touched" only in this case.
    2. The EXACT duplicate of a story (api) and of a rule (engine) compares the title with the escape decoded, before the usual case folding and NFD.
    3. A tool refusal that says "exige X" gains, at the same point, the KEYS that arrived (names, never values), or that a string/list arrived instead of an object. emit_artifact with a key missing in the payload states the missing keys and the received ones, instead of {:missing_keys, …}.
    4. complete_story names exactly what is missing (falta: business_rule_ids), by the same yardstick as the api's missingForReady, read from the returned story.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/argumentos_de_ferramenta.ex:88 (decodificar_escapes), :135 (chaves_recebidas), :152 (com_chaves_recebidas); apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tools/complete_story.ex:80 (faltam); apps/api/src/domain/backlog/story-overlap.ts:39 (decodificarEscapesLiterais), :25 (normalizarTitulo); apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/artifact_dedupe.ex (normalizar)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/argumentos_de_ferramenta_test.exs (describe "RN-725": escape decoded, text without \u and list intact; refusal with the keys and without the values; complete_story names the field); apps/api/test/domain/backlog/story-overlap.spec.ts (escaped title matches the normal one; a different title stays different)
    • Origin: AT-405, AT-408

    RN-726 — The provider's exhausted credit pauses the dev agent, with origin infra, without burning the following tasks

    • Rule: when the model's provider refuses for lack of CREDIT (HTTP 402, or OpenRouter's text "available credits"/"add credits"), the dev agent's turn is not "stopped without concluding": the task is blocked with the reason "crédito do provider esgotado" and origin infra (never codigo), the automatic stop counter (RN-047) does NOT advance, and the agent does NOT claim the next task — it stays in idle_tripped and emits dev.credit_exhausted (motivo: credito_esgotado), never dev.idle_tripped. Resumption is the Rearmar that already existed in the team panel, after topping up the credit; the blocked task comes back through the usual unblock. The Executores screen shows the agent travado with "crédito do provedor do modelo esgotado — recarregue e rearme para retomar", and the bell says the same. The same recognition makes FalhaDeTurno.origem/1 return infra for the credit final frame (it applies to the gate agents, like QA). The session counts dev.credit_exhausted as pending work, like dev.idle_tripped. Since RN-730 the 402 arrives with its own code (insufficient_credit) and it is that which decides; recognition by text remained only as a safety net.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/falha_de_turno.ex:134 (credito_esgotado?), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:906 (handle_outcome), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/agent_io.ex:271 (pausar_por_credito), apps/web/src/lib/agent-status.ts:198 (breakerReasonFor), apps/api/src/application/use-cases/sessions/get-session-pending-work.use-case.ts
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/dev_agent_server_test.exs ("402 do provider: origem infra, pausa sem idle_tripped e sem queimar a próxima task"; failure: "erro que não é de crédito segue como antes: parou sem concluir, conta no disjuntor"), apps/engine/test/engine/agents/falha_de_turno_test.exs ("402 do provider é infra e é reconhecido"), apps/web/src/lib/agent-status.test.ts ("RN-726: dev.credit_exhausted é travado com motivo de crédito…")
    • Origin: AT-407 (TP-01 of 10/03: OpenRouter's 402 became three tasks blocked with origin codigo and the automatic stop)

    RN-731 — The turn's closing states what was recorded with the server's number

    • Rule: the number of what a conversational agent recorded in a turn is a SERVER FACT, never model text (RN-163). The Criativo, the PO and the Arquiteto count, in the turn's process, each write tool that came back ok (emit_artifact by type — business rule, decision, note, other —, create_epic, create_story, update_story, archive_story, create_task), and the closing of agent.response gains, AFTER the model's text and without rewriting it, the line "Gravado neste turno: N …". A refused tool and a read do not count; a turn without writes gets no line; the count dies with the turn. The line comes out in the turn's language (RN-622): the author's, otherwise the project's; pt* in Portuguese ("Gravado neste turno: 1 regra de negócio"), any other language — or none resolved — in English ("Recorded this turn: 2 business rules"), with singular and plural in both.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/gravado_no_turno.ex:62 (anotar), :95 (descarregar), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/idioma_da_resposta.ex:272 (idioma_do_turno), apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/texto_do_turno.ex:65 (payload_do_turno)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/agents/gravado_no_turno_test.exs (happy: six rules and one decision recorded, one refused — the model's "8 regras" closing stays and gains "6 regras de negócio, 1 decisão" next to it; no writes: nothing added; pt-BR and en, singular and plural; a language outside the two falls back to en), apps/engine/test/engine/agents/criativo_server_test.exs
    • Origin: AT-414 (TP-01 of 10/03: the Criativo closed with "Registrei as 8 regras de negócio" and the panel had 6)

    RN-732 — The turn's text goes through the same escape yardstick as the arguments

    • Rule: the text the model writes in a conversational agent's turn — the content (closing) and each passos of agent.response — goes through the SAME decodificar_escapes/1 of RN-725 before being recorded: a literal ã becomes ã. One yardstick only, without a copy, and with the same limit: it only acts when there is a literal \uXXXX, so text without escapes — including code with a literal "\n" in a fenced block — comes back byte for byte. The streaming agent.delta is NOT decoded: an escape may arrive split between two deltas, and the recorded bubble replaces the in-progress text at the end of the turn. A rule of its own, and not a clause of RN-725, because the surface is different (model response, not a tool argument) and the decision about the delta is this one's.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/texto_do_turno.ex:65 (payload_do_turno), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/argumentos_de_ferramenta.ex:88 (decodificar_escapes)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/agents/texto_do_turno_test.exs (happy: the five measured escapes, in the closing and in a step; intact: text without escapes and a code block with a literal \n)
    • Origin: AT-417 (TP-01 of 10/03, 2nd round: the Criativo's final answer recorded with "colisão", "usuário" and three more escapes in the bubble)

    RN-733 — The credit failure says what to do, and the reason is never the tuple

    • Rule: the reason recorded in the agent.error of the conversational agents, the Infra Lead and the ToolLoop (dev agent) is the TEXT of the final frame when there is one — never the inspected {:final, …} tuple; other shapes keep inspect/1. With the provider's credit exhausted (errorCode: insufficient_credit, RN-730, or the text recognition of RN-726), the bubble's mensagem is short and actionable in the turn's language (RN-622): pt "Crédito do provedor do modelo esgotado — recarregue a chave e tente de novo.", any other language "The model provider's credit is exhausted — top up the key and try again."; with no resolved language, pt-BR. The provider's JSON stays only in the reason (diagnosis). The other errors keep the usual sentence.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/falha_de_turno.ex:158 (mensagem), :181 (diagnostico)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/agents/dev_lead_server_test.exs (402 in pt-BR and en: short sentence, reason without {:final; upstream error: usual sentence), apps/engine/test/engine/agents/falha_de_turno_test.exs
    • Origin: AT-421 (TP-01 of 10/03, 2nd round: the Dev Lead recorded reason: "{:final, …" and the bubble dumped the 402's JSON)

    RN-735 — The none network of the Arquiteto's image says it does not install dependencies

    • Rule: the description of choose_project_image and its RESULT say that, with network none, no package manager (npm, pnpm, pip, mix, cargo, go mod...) installs a dependency in the container, and that the Infra inherits that network (RN-723). With none, the result asks the model to call the tool again with egress if the stack installs dependencies. It is a NOTICE, never a refusal: the api does not guess the stack, and the broker's containment (ADR 0130) does not change.
    • Code: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tools/choose_project_image.ex:96 (mensagem_de_fixada), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tools/choose_project_image.ex:23 (spec)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/tools/choose_project_image_test.exs
    • Origin: AT-420 (TP-01 of 10/03: node:22-bookworm-slim with network none, and the dev agent could not npm install)

    RN-742 — The dev agent does not die silently, and a late HTTP response does not stay in its mailbox

    • Rule: every non-streamed HTTP call from the engine to the api (post_returning/3 and the GETs of EngineApiClient.Live) runs in a disposable Task; a response that arrives after the receive_timeout lands in a process that has already ended, never in the caller's mailbox, and an exception inside the request comes back as {:error, _}. And a dev agent that CRASHES (an exit that is neither :normal nor :shutdown) is not treated as finished: Engine.Dev.Monitor records a DURABLE agent.error with origin infra (reason: processo_do_dev_caiu, religado, taskId) and RESTARTS it from its own dev_agent_states row, up to 3 times per agent; the resume goes through the handle_continue({:restart_recovery, _}) that already existed, which blocks the interrupted task with a diagnosis (origin infra) and tries the next claim. Without a row, or at the ceiling, the row is removed and the error says there was no restart. llm_turn_stream/6 stays out of the isolation (the into: writes into the caller's dictionary); the conversational agents already call it from inside the turn's Task (TurnoAssincrono), so the "HTTP inside the GenServer" pattern does not reach them — declared. The web did not change: the Executores tab now sees the recovery's agent.error and dev.blocked.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/sessions/engine_api_client.ex:2073 (isolado), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/monitor.ex:69 (ao_terminar), :107 (registrar_e_religar)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/sessions/engine_api_client_isolado_test.exs (a late message does not reach the caller; an exception becomes {:error, _}), apps/engine/test/engine/dev/monitor_test.exs (a crash records an infra agent.error and restarts; :normal deletes the row; without a row it does not restart)
    • Origin: AT-428 (10/08: CaseClauseError in Finch.HTTP1.Conn.receive_response/8 after the npm install timeout)

    RN-743 — Blocking the task preserves the worktree's work, and the next one starts from it

    • Rule: before blocking a task (any origin, including the taskBudgetMicros budget ceiling, whose VALUE does not change here), what the dev agent left in the worktree becomes a commit on the task's own branch, with the identity <agente>[bot] (git add -A + commit --no-verify). The dev.blocked carries trabalhoPreservado ({branch, commit} or null) and the diagnosis STATES where the work was left — or that it could not be preserved, with the reason; a failure to preserve never prevents the block. The SAME agent's next task (therefore, of the same module) is born from that branch (create_from/4) instead of dev, once only. A clean worktree produces no commit. Declared: the pointer lives only in memory (an engine restart loses the pointer, not the commit), and in runner mode the worktree is on the user's machine and the engine preserves nothing.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/worktree_manager.ex:72 (preservar_em), :55 (create_from), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/agent_io.ex:491 (preservar_trabalho), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/agent_io.ex:321 (base_preservada)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/worktree_manager_test.exs (preservar_em/3 e create_from/4: commit with dev-api[bot] and the next task sees the file; clean is :nada; a folder without a repository is an error), apps/engine/test/engine/dev/bloqueio_preserva_trabalho_test.exs (the dev.blocked states the branch; a failure to preserve does not prevent the block)
    • Origin: AT-429 (10/08: feature/task-86dade03 without a commit, package.json and src lost after the budget block)

    RN-744 — The dev knows the shell is sh and starts by seeing its own worktree

    • Rule: the terminal tool's description says the container's shell is sh (dash), not bash — no brace expansion, no [[ ]], no arrays, no source — and gives the form that works. Each dev agent task's kickoff gets a second pinned message with the worktree's SNAPSHOT: the branch and the files that already exist (tracked + untracked not ignored), with a ceiling of 80 lines and the real total when it cuts (ADR 0060); it is the server that assembles it, with no parameter from the model. A folder the engine cannot reach (runner mode) says that the snapshot is unavailable and says to list through terminal. Declared and NOT fixed: the dev's worktree may be born before the infra PR's merge, so what the Infra delivered through a PR may not be on the dev it starts from.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tools/terminal.ex:12 (spec), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/worktree_manager.ex:108 (retrato), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:595 (retrato_do_worktree)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/worktree_manager_test.exs (retrato/2: branch and files; an unreachable folder says unavailable; the terminal description says sh)
    • Origin: AT-444 (TP-01 of 10/08: mkdir -p src/{a,b} in dash and the dev recreating what already existed)

    RN-745 — In a cut answer, only the incomplete call is refused

    • Rule: when the model's answer comes cut by the output ceiling (truncated: true, RN-737), only the LAST tool call of the answer is treated as incomplete and refused with the named error (which now says "the last one of the answer", that the earlier ones were executed and asks for "one per call"). The earlier calls of the same answer execute normally — measured in the code: the OpenAI-compatible stream assembles the calls in order, the cut falls on the last one, and the api's parseArgumentos returns {} for the broken JSON. The call is identified by name and arguments. A cut answer without a known call still refuses every tool (RN-737's yardstick). The description of propose_adr steers one ADR per answer.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/resposta_cortada.ex:44 (registrar), :64 (incompleta?), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/argumentos_de_ferramenta.ex:26 (executar), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tools/propose_adr.ex:27 (spec)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/resposta_cortada_test.exs ("só a última chamada da resposta cortada é recusada; as completas executam"; "ferramenta de resposta cortada NÃO executa", without a known call)
    • Origin: AT-430 (10/08: the Arquiteto sent 5 propose_adr in a cut answer and all five were refused; 0 ADRs)

    RN-746 — The Arquiteto's closing states what it recorded, and refused writes are counted

    • Rule: the factual line "Gravado neste turno: …" of RN-731 now counts the Arquiteto's writes (proposed ADR, module map, module assignment, decided image, C4 diagram, routing to infra, module contract, insight), the Staff's RFC and the UX Designer's prototype, and counts separately the refused WRITE calls ({:error, _}) of the turn: "…; N chamadas de escrita recusadas". A turn that tried to write and recorded nothing closes with "Gravado neste turno: nada; N chamadas de escrita recusadas." (en: "Recorded this turn: nothing; N write calls refused."). The Criativo now notes refusals too; Staff and UX Designer now note them. A read never counts. The model's text is not rewritten (RN-163: the agent does not announce as done what the server refused — the reader sees the fact next to it). Out, declared: Dev Lead (the plan suspends the turn, RN-284) and Infra Lead.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/gravado_no_turno.ex:77 (anotar), :96 (descarregar), :141 (classe), apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/arquiteto_server.ex:323 (dispatch_tool)

    RN-756 — The Anamnese only runs with the subject's technical-content interaction; approving does not count

    • Rule: revises the "own interaction" count of RN-680/RN-722. Only these count toward making someone a subject of the round: (a) an actor_kind: "user" event of the member themselves whose type is in tipos_de_evento_com_conteudo/0 — chat.message (a message written by them) and chat.structured_question_answered — and (b) an action decision with a written refusal reason (non-empty rejectionReason, a correction). Approval, handoff acceptance, a click and any other user event do NOT count: they are not evidence of competence. The floor of 5 (RN-722), the daily ceiling and the no-subject outcome (reason in the log; anamnese.run_skipped only in a manually requested round) do not change, and triage stays LOCAL, before the LLM and the RAG.
    • Code: apps/engine/lib/engine/anamnese/elegibilidade.ex:61 (avaliar), :102 (recusa_com_motivo?), :108 (tipos_de_evento_com_conteudo)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/anamnese/elegibilidade_test.exs (só interação com conteúdo técnico conta (RN-756, AT-439): a message and a structured answer make a subject; only approvals, handoff and refusal without a reason do not)
    • Origin: AT-439 (TP-01, 10/08, project c1dc053d: two rounds during execution, US$ 0.20, both skip_proficiency with "o usuário apenas aprovou propostas")

    RN-757 — The auto-approved terminal waits for the command, and the command ceiling is 2 min

    • Rule: the terminal action the api auto-approves is EXECUTED inside the same propose_action request (api → engine /internal/actions/execute → api container-exec → broker), and the engine waits for that request for TERMINAL_ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS + 120 s — not for the Req default of 15 s. The command ceiling (TERMINAL_ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS) goes from 15 s to 120 s in the three composes and in runtime.exs. The chain becomes broker (T) < api → broker (T + up to 45 s, RN-604) < engine → container-exec (T + 90 s) < engine → propose_action (T + 120 s), and with T = 120 s the outer hop (240 s) stays below the 300 s of headers of Node's fetch. If the wait still overflows, the model receives NAMED text — the ceiling in seconds, that the process may keep running and the suggestion of nohup … & with a log —, never a raw %Req.TransportError{reason: :timeout}. Asynchronous/streaming exec was considered and not done: the smallest change that makes a common npm install fit is the ceiling.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/sessions/engine_api_client.ex:1919 (opcoes_do_propose_action) and apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/hooks/action_pipeline.ex:80 (resultado_de_teto_do_terminal)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/sessions/engine_api_client_propose_terminal_test.exs (a fake api that takes 16 s is still waited for; order of the ceilings below 300 s; the overflow text names the ceiling)
    • Origin: AT-440 (TP-01 of 10/08: npm install 2>&1 | tail -50 in a node:22-bookworm-slim container came back "falha no pipeline: %Req.TransportError{reason: :timeout}"; measured on this machine, the same npm install of bcrypt+sqlite3+express took 5.3 s with the image in cache and prebuilds available — the chain, not the command, cut at 15 s)

    RN-760 — The dev agent's next task starts from its last not-yet-merged branch

    • Rule: generalizes RN-743. On finishing a task — approved in the gates (nextAction: "done") or blocked for ANY reason, including the "ciclo de correção esgotado (gate)" that the api decides and reaches the engine only as gate_resolved —, the dev agent keeps its branch and the SAME agent's next task (therefore, of the same module) is born from that branch, and not from dev. What was left uncommitted in the worktree is preserved first, as in RN-743; the pointer recorded by block_task/4 is not overwritten. When the previous branch is already an ancestor of the local dev (merged), the next one starts from dev, which already contains it. WHEN the queue moves does not change: the next task is still claimed at the end of the gates, before the user's merge (owner decision). Declared: a squash merge on a remote does not leave the branch an ancestor of dev, so the next one keeps starting from it; the pointer still lives only in memory, and in runner mode the merge check does not run.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/agent_io.ex:296 (levar_branch_para_a_proxima), :324 (criar_worktree), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/worktree_manager.ex:199 (ja_mergeada_volta_ao_trabalho)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/bloqueio_preserva_trabalho_test.exs (levar_branch_para_a_proxima/1 e criar_worktree/2: approved carries the branch to the next one; the block's pointer is not overwritten), apps/engine/test/engine/dev/worktree_manager_test.exs (add_worktree/4 com a branch da task anterior: not merged starts from it, merged starts from dev)
    • Origin: AT-447 (TP-01 of 10/09: tasks 762833bd and 5ebad64b — the second was born from dev without the first's login route, and QA failed it)

    RN-763 — Closing the execution session stops the dev agents and the gates, and "Parar execução" in the Executores tab

    • Rule: closing the session (session.closed/closed_abnormally, through SessionLifecycleWorker, the point every closing goes through) STOPS its dev agents on all nodes and the project's gates (QA lead and SecOps) — the yardstick that already applied to the conversational agents (RN-581). The process is brought down immediately, without recording the turn in progress, and Engine.Dev.Monitor is warned first (esquecer/1): the durable row is removed and the agent is NOT restarted (RN-742). For each dev with a task in progress (working, awaiting_approval or awaiting_gate), the worktree's work becomes a commit on the task's branch (RN-743) and the task is BLOCKED with reason "sessão de execução encerrada", origin politica, and the diagnosis saying where the work was left — it goes back to the queue when a human releases it, never lost. The session's gate_states rows are removed, so the GateRescuer does not reopen a cycle of the closed session. The Executores tab gets "Parar execução" (role developer, that of closing a session), whose mechanism is the SAME closing (closing → closed), without a new state; the confirmation says before the click what remains: the tasks in progress (with the count), the open PRs, the pending approvals and that the session does not reopen. Declared: a dev agent's pending action stays pending; an open PR stays open; a session with execution.activated does not reopen (RN-650), so resuming means activating execution in a new session; the confirmation's task count comes from the tab's event window. The confirmation on the session bar's "Encerrar" belongs to another activity (AT-452).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/encerramento_da_sessao.ex:40 (parar_da_sessao), :74 (parar_processos), :104 (devolver_task), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/monitor.ex:34 (esquecer), apps/engine/lib/engine/workers/session_lifecycle_worker.ex:94 (parar_execucao), apps/web/src/components/PararExecucao.tsx
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/encerramento_da_sessao_test.exs (the dev dies without being restarted, the row is removed, the task is blocked with origin politica; a session without a dev is a no-op and a dev of another session stays up), apps/web/src/components/PararExecucao.test.tsx (the confirmation says what remains and closes closing → closed; the api's refusal stays in the confirmation; without the role the button is inert with the reason)
    • Origin: AT-456 (TP-01 of 10/09: with the execution session closed, the dev-encurtador-api blocked task 3, took 4 and opened a PR; the only brake was stopping the container)

    RN-765 — The QA gate judges the TASK, and a sibling task's RF is an observation

    • Rule: QA-strategy (test plan, RN-674) and QA-automation receive, besides the story, the task's CUT: title and description, the story's business rules and the OTHER tasks of the same story with their status (siblingTasks in GET .../dev-context). The verdict is about what belongs to THIS task: a requirement that belongs to a sibling task (done or pending) does not fail it — it goes to resumo as an observation, outside itens, and does not prevent approved. The yardstick is PROMPT, not code: emit_qa_verdict still requires only a green suite to approve. Since AT-479, when the task description or a rule quantifies PER item ("por X", "cada", "de todos os" — "por exemplo"/"por padrão" do not count), the cut gains the section "Forma da resposta": check that the answer carries the value of EACH item, not only the total (the dashboard approved with only {totalCliques} over "a contagem… de todos os links"), and that the input refusal names the field — the latter is an OBSERVATION in resumo, outside itens, unless a rule requires it. It remains a prompt yardstick: no new failure in code.

    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:18 (texto), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:82 (secao_da_forma), apps/api/src/application/use-cases/execution/get-dev-task-context.use-case.ts:195 (tarefasIrmas) and apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_automacao_agent.ex:170

    RN-772 — The Arquiteto reads the CURRENT status of the ADRs it proposed

    • Rule: the Arquiteto has the read listar_adrs_propostas: the PROJECT's open_adr_pr actions, read now from proposed_actions (same Postgres, no HTTP in the loop), with each one's status — pending, approved, auto-approved, executed (with the PR URL), denied (with the reason) or failed. Contained (ADR 0060, RN-164): no parameter, scope closed to the context's project, one count and one query per call, the 20 most recent, and the real total stated when it cuts. A read failure is tool.result ok: false with the reason, never empty text. Since RN-784 it IS in the Jev's floor.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tools/listar_adrs_propostas.ex:37 (run), :40 (ler), apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/arquiteto_server.ex:124 (ListarAdrsPropostas)

    RN-773 — The Arquiteto reads the backlog, and checks before asserting state

    • Rule: the Arquiteto gains the same listar_backlog as the PO (RN-164), with the task count per story. Its kickoff and the propose_adr result say that the ADRs' status and the tasks change outside the conversation and say to check both reads before asserting them, instead of the snapshot of an earlier result. No team flow is narrated here (RN-711).
