    • Origin: AT-454 (TP-01 of 10/09: with 17 tasks in the backlog, the Arquiteto said "faltam as tarefas" and "não consigo verificá-las daqui")

    RN-774 — A primeira tarefa de cada módulo tem o dobro do teto por tarefa

    A primeira tarefa de um módulo monta o esqueleto inteiro (manifesto, app, banco, testes) e custa mais que as seguintes: no TP-01 de 08/10 ela gastou US$ 0,53 com o Haiku 4.5 e foi bloqueada pelo teto fixo de US$ 0,50 (taskBudgetMicros: 500000). Por decisão do dono (09/10), ela ganha 2× o teto por tarefa (US$ 1,00 com o padrão); as demais seguem o teto configurado. O multiplicador é constante nomeada no engine, não configuração.

    "Primeira" é medida pelo log, nunca pelo estado da tarefa: é a tarefa cujo backlog.task_claimed é o MAIS ANTIGO do projeto para aquele módulo. Bloquear zera assigned_to e reiniciar o engine perde a memória do processo; o log não muda, então a mesma tarefa reivindicada de novo depois de liberada continua a primeira, e uma correção pedida por gate também recebe o 2×. A api responde task.primeiraDoModulo no contexto do dev (false sem module, e false sem claim nenhum do módulo — o teto normal é o conservador), e o teto EFETIVO é resolvido num ponto só do engine. O bloqueio por orçamento diz o teto que valeu para aquela tarefa ("teto da primeira tarefa do módulo: 1000000 micro-USD, US$ 1.00"). Os gates de QA seguem com o task_budget_micros base — fora do recorte do card.

    • Onde: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/teto_da_tarefa.ex:32 (efetivo); apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:669 (diagnostico_de_orcamento); apps/api/src/application/use-cases/execution/get-dev-task-context.use-case.ts:182 (ehPrimeiraTarefaDoModulo)
    • Teste: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/dev_agent_server_test.exs (describe "RN-774": a primeira com US$ 0,53 não bloqueia, a segunda do mesmo módulo bloqueia — caso de falha —, a primeira de outro módulo também ganha 2×); apps/engine/test/engine/dev/teto_da_tarefa_test.exs; apps/api/test/application/use-cases/execution/get-dev-task-context.use-case.spec.ts (describe "RN-774")
    • Origem: AT-433

    RN-778 — The orphan dev agent closes through a new event at boot

    • Rule: at boot, for each non-terminal session, the dev agent whose last dev.* in the session is a waiting one (dev.working, dev.blocked, dev.idle_tripped and the others GetSessionPendingWorkUseCase counts) and that has NO row in dev_agent_states IN THIS session gets a dev.error with origin infra (reason: dev_agent_sem_processo) followed by dev.idle, which takes it out of the pending work yardstick (RN-064). It is the mold of RN-586 for the dev.* vocabulary: it never re-executes, never rewrites an event, and the durable row is the proof of ownership — with it the DevRehydrator restarts the agent, and closing it would be killing a healthy agent. It does not touch the task: a blocked task or one with a PR waiting for the merge keeps holding the current execution session through RN-776 (AT-457), which has a human owner.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_orfao.ex:49 (varrer), apps/engine/lib/engine/sessions/rehydrator.ex:81 (varrer)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/dev_orfao_test.exs (the orphan closes with infra dev.error + dev.idle; the one with a durable row and the one already idle do not; a failing read closes nothing)
    • Origin: AT-465 (TP-01 of 10/09: session 63a4e1aa rescheduled by the heartbeat every 30 s by a dev.working without a process)

    RN-779 — The worktree of the (re)claimed task starts from the CURRENT dev and integrates it

    • Rule: before creating a task's worktree (first time or resumed), the engine runs git fetch origin in the project's working tree (serialized per project) — it is on the remote that PRs are merged, and the local dev only moved at the checkout of initialization. The tip of dev is origin/dev when the local one fell behind. The task is born: (1) from the previous task's unmerged branch (RN-760), (2) from its OWN branch when it has work that dev does not contain — the task resumed in a new process, which lost the in-memory pointer (RN-743) —, or (3) from the current tip of dev. In cases (1) and (2) the worktree INTEGRATES the tip of dev (merge --no-ff, identity <agente>[bot]) before the first step. A conflict in that integration is a NAMED refusal ("conflito ao integrar a dev atual", with the files), the merge is aborted, the source branch stays intact and the task blocks with its own reason and origin codigo. A failing fetch blocks the task with git's reason, never falls back to the old dev. Readoption after a merge conflict (RN-715) does not integrate on its own. Measured in TP-01: AT-447 FIRED on the 1st claim (reflog: Created from feature/task-b3cc92b8); what lost the base was the reclaim, which reset the branch to the LOCAL dev stuck at the infra merge (Reset to dev). Declared: in runner mode none of this runs (the git is the user's machine's).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/worktree_manager.ex:151 (atualizar_remoto), :195 (add_worktree), :227 (ponta_do_trabalho), :225 (retomada), :300 (integrar_trabalho)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/worktree_manager_test.exs (integração da dev atual (RN-779): resumed with an old branch starts from the remote's new dev; preserved work integrates the new dev; a previous one in awaiting_user is the base with dev integrated; a conflict is a named refusal)
    • Origin: AT-458 (TP-01 of 10/09: feature/task-c80daf6c was born from 1fa2241 without the 12 tasks merged afterwards, and the merge conflicted)

    RN-780 — A reasoning-only answer cut by the ceiling is a cut, not giving up

    • Rule: in the ToolLoop (the path of every execution and gate agent), an answer without text and without a tool call that the api marks truncated: true (RN-737's finish_reason: length) is reasoning that ate the output ceiling. The round is retried ONCE, without the empty answer in the history and counting an iteration — the iteration ceiling and the task budget still apply. If it repeats, the loop stops marking corte_pelo_teto, and the dev agent blocks the task with the reason "resposta só de raciocínio, cortada pelo teto" and origin modelo, instead of "parou sem concluir nem reportar bloqueio". A tool call in between resets the retry. Declared: the engine receives from the api truncated and usage.outputTokens, but NOT the reasoningTokens (they stay only in token_usage), so an empty answer that hits the ceiling without finish_reason: length is not recognized.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tool_loop.ex:219 (parada_sem_chamada), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:1116 (motivo_da_parada), :1119 (stop_diagnosis)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/tool_loop_test.exs (corte só de raciocínio: retried once and goes on; repeated stops with corte_pelo_teto), apps/engine/test/engine/dev/dev_agent_server_test.exs (resposta só de raciocínio cortada pelo teto bloqueia com motivo nomeado)
    • Origin: AT-459 (TP-01 of 10/09: task c80daf6c blocked in 40 s with 8,192 output tokens, all of them reasoning)

    RN-781 — The dev's commit message does not record a pending item that belongs to the system

    • Rule: the report_done summary is the dev agent's commit message, and commit, push and PR are done by the system after it. Before proposing the git_commit, the engine cuts from the pending marker ("Pendente", "Pendência", "Pending") to the end when the rest speaks of commit, push or PR, and removes the parenthesis "(ainda sem commit…)"; a pending item that does not speak of that stays. The description of the summary field says it becomes the commit message and that commit, push and PR are not narrated. A summary that ends up empty falls back to the default message <agente>: <task>.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/agent_io.ex:374 (mensagem_de_commit), :373 (propose_commit), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/tools/report_done.ex:27 (summary)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/mensagem_de_commit_test.exs (cuts the narration; removes the parenthesis; a pending item unrelated to the system stays)
    • Origin: AT-464 item 2 (TP-01 of 10/09: git log feature/task-9d6dcd53 with "Pendente: commit… as ações git_commit, git_push e pr_open não estavam disponíveis neste passo")

    RN-787 — The gate counts the PR's diff against the origin's dev, and the QA verdict records a list

    • Rule: (1) the diff that QA and SecOps judge (dev...HEAD, RN-664) uses the NEWEST dev the working tree sees: with a local origin (a path on disk) the gate first runs git fetch origin dev, best effort, and between origin/dev and the local dev the one that contains the other wins. The clone's local dev does not move when the PR is merged at the origin, and the SecOps verdict counted the accumulation of the already merged tasks. With a remote origin there is no fetch in the gate (the credential belongs to Workspace) — declared. The semgrep and gitleaks keep scanning the whole worktree, not only the diff: narrowing what the scanner sees changes what the gate fails, and that is left to the owner. (2) itens and coverageMatrix of the QA verdict reach artifact.qa_verdict always as a LIST: a JSON string of a list is decoded (the mold of RN-719), and a string that is not a list becomes [].
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/diff.ex:44 (base_mais_nova), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/hooks/termination.ex:46 (lista)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/diff_test.exs (task B born from A, with A merged at the origin: the diff is only B) and apps/engine/test/engine/gates/hooks/termination_test.exs (a JSON string becomes a list; a list intact; an invalid string becomes [])
    • Origin: AT-464 items 1 and 3 (TP-01 of 10/09, session 80e3ce09: "17 arquivo(s) alterado(s) nesta PR" in a PR of 3 files against dev, and coverageMatrix/itens recorded as "[]" in the last verdicts)

    RN-790 — The README on how to start the deliverable is a DoD criterion the PO writes

    • Rule: the PO's kickoff instruction says to include, in the DoD of the story that creates the application's entry point (server, CLI, configuration or persistence) — or in a story of its own —, the criterion "README diz como instalar, configurar (variáveis de ambiente obrigatórias e opcionais, com o padrão) e subir a aplicação, e como criar o primeiro acesso, quando houver". The dev agent fulfills the story's DoD, and the gate judges by it. A PROMPT yardstick, like RN-765: no tool refuses a story without that criterion. The other variant — the dev agent updating the README on every task that touches configuration/entry point — was NOT done: it would require classifying the task in AmbienteDoAgente or in report_done, a new code rule — declared.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/po_server.ex:560 (kickoff_instruction)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/agents/po_server_test.exs:438 (the kickoff instruction carries the README criterion)
    • Origin: AT-466 (TP-01 of 10/09: encurtador-api came out with a README only with npm test, without the mandatory JWT_SECRET, DB_PATH/PORT, npm start nor how to create the first member)

    RN-792 — On declaring the contract, the Arquiteto receives the stories' routes that the contract does not declare

    • Rule: after declare_module_contracts records the version (RN-684), the tool reads the project's backlog and extracts, deterministically and without an LLM, every MÉTODO /caminho (GET, POST, PUT, PATCH, DELETE) from the title, description, RF and DoD of the non-archived stories, and the same pattern from the contract's assinaturas. A route cited by a story and absent from the UNION of the contract's routes (the front story cites the back route) comes back in the result as a DIVERGENCE, with what the contract declares for the same method — loop input (RN-163), for the Arquiteto to fix the contract or record the decision. Parameter (:id, {id}), trailing slash and query string are normalized; a ceiling of 10 lines, with the total. A failing backlog read does NOT refuse the declaration (already recorded): the result says it did not check. Left out, declared: a route only the contract declares (not a divergence), a story edited AFTER the declaration (only checked on the next one), and the contract following a route the DEV renamed (QA names the divergence in the verdict, RN-786, but nothing takes it back to the Arquiteto).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/rotas_do_contrato.ex:37 (divergencias), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tools/declare_module_contracts.ex:119 (conferencia_com_historias)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/tools/declare_module_contracts_test.exs (GET /painel in the story × GET /panel in the contract flags; an equal route with normalized parameter and slash does not flag; an unreadable backlog says it did not check)
    • Origin: AT-468 (TP-01 of 10/09: the story asked for GET /painel, the contract declared GET /panel, and nobody flagged it in the architecture)

    RN-796 — The gitleaks item states the rule and the excerpt with the secret masked

    • Rule: the item gitleaks puts in the SecOps verdict carries, besides the path and the line, the rule that flagged it (RuleID) and the flagged excerpt (Match) with the secret (Secret) replaced by *** — never the secret in clear. Without the Secret to mask, the excerpt does not go in. And the execution agent's environment (RN-706) says in one line that a test password, token or key is generated at runtime, never as a literal. No new allowlist.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/actions/gitleaks_detector.ex:121 (mensagem), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/ambiente_do_agente.ex:102 (montar)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/actions/gitleaks_relatorio_test.exs, apps/engine/test/engine/harness/ambiente_do_agente_test.exs
    • Origin: AT-470

    RN-797 — A task whose branch has no diff against dev closes as "already in dev"

    • Rule: on report_done, before proposing commit, push and PR, the dev agent asks whether the worktree's branch carries something dev does not have: a clean tree and git diff --quiet <ponta da dev>...HEAD with no difference. It is the case of the reintegrated task (RN-779) whose code already came in through another PR. Without a diff, the task closes as done with the dev.task_already_in_dev event (agent, task, branch, reason) — no commit, no PR, no gates and no click. Any doubt (a folder the engine cannot reach, as in runner mode; git that fails; missing dev ref) is "has a diff": the task goes on to the gates, it never closes on its own. It measures DIFF and not ancestry, so it also covers the squash merge that RN-760 declares it does not see. Declared: the branch's old PR, if it exists, is not closed on the provider.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/worktree_manager.ex:246 (sem_diff_contra_a_dev?), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:1048 (ja_na_dev)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/tarefa_ja_na_dev_test.exs
    • Origin: AT-474

    RN-798 — QA checks the commands of the README touched by the delivery against the scripts

    • Rule: when the delivery's diff against dev touches a README, QA checks, without an LLM, that every npm run <s>, npm test, npm start, pnpm [run] <s> and yarn [run] <s> cited in it exists in scripts of the package.json of the same folder (otherwise the one at the worktree root). The check enters consolidation as one more verdict ("Conferência do README"): a divergence is changes_requested with one item per command, naming the missing script; with no divergence, it approves. Without a README in the diff, a diff that failed or an unreachable package.json, nothing is asserted. OUT, declared: the manager's built-in subcommand (pnpm install, pnpm dlx…), a command with a flag before the script (pnpm --filter x test), the project's own CLI flags and a command that is not Node's.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/conferencia_do_readme.ex:28 (conferir), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_lead_server.ex:776 (conferencia_do_readme)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/conferencia_do_readme_test.exs
    • Origin: AT-475

    RN-805 — The recorded test plan is the one the tool accepted

    • Rule: emit_plano_de_teste validates the ALREADY normalized arguments (RN-719: criteriosExecutaveis in a JSON string becomes a list), and the hook that ends the QA-strategy's loop reads criteriosExecutaveis through the SAME normalization (Termination.lista/1, that of RN-787). Before, the tool said "plano registrado: 9 critérios", the hook carried the raw string, the artifact was refused (:criterios_vazios), the agent returned {:ok, plano} anyway and the Automação fell into the string's Enum — the QA Lead died and the gate stayed stopped until the rescue (18 min in real use). A refused artifact is now a NAMED failure of the QA-strategy (agent.error, origin codigo) and the review goes on without a plan, as it already did when the plan fails; the Automação also normalizes the criteria it receives.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/hooks/termination_plano_de_teste.ex:23 (criterios_executaveis), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_estrategia_agent.ex:231 (emit_returning)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/qa_estrategia_agent_test.exs ("critérios em string JSON viram lista: o plano é gravado e devolvido como lista")
    • Origin: AT-478 (TP-01 of 10/10)

    RN-806 — One QA cycle per task: a request prior to the verdict is discarded

    • Rule: the QA Lead runs ONE cycle per task. A {:run, task} request for the task whose cycle is suspended waiting for a decision is ignored, and at the end of a cycle (verdict or block) every {:run, task} that was waiting in the process's mailbox is discarded — it was requested before the verdict existed, and the dev's legitimate correction is only born after it. In real use the rescue (RN-722) fired the cycle twice at the same instant, and the second, running after the approved one, recorded a contrary verdict about the already merged task. OUT, declared: two engine replicas (the mailbox is per process, the same caveat as the GateRescuer) and the cause of the double sweep of the rescue, which was not fixed here.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_lead_server.ex:106 (handle_cast), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_lead_server.ex:157 (descartar_pedidos_obsoletos)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/qa_lead_server_test.exs ("pedido repetido que esperava na caixa é descartado ao fim do ciclo"; "pedido para a task com ciclo suspenso não abre um segundo")
    • Origin: AT-478 (TP-01 of 10/10)

    RN-810 — A transient provider network failure is retried, and its block is infra without counting in the breaker

    • Rule: when the call to the model comes back with a provider NETWORK error — the connection/timeout code the api normalizes (ADR 0041) or, without a code, the transport text (EAI_AGAIN, ECONNRESET, ETIMEDOUT, ECONNREFUSED, ENOTFOUND) —, the ToolLoop retries it up to twice, with waits of 1 s and 3 s, recording toolloop.network_retry each time. It applies to every consumer of the ToolLoop (dev agents and gate subagents). Once the ceiling is exhausted, the outcome's origin is infra, never codigo, and the task's block of the dev agent for that cause does NOT increment the consecutive blocks counter (RN-047): the agent keeps claiming. In real use a 17 s getaddrinfo EAI_AGAIN openrouter.ai blocked two tasks as codigo and tripped the dev.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tool_loop.ex:246 (llm_turn_com_retentativa), apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/falha_de_turno.ex:112 (falha_de_rede?), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:952 (bloquear_parada), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/agent_io.ex:228 (finish_task)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/tool_loop_test.exs ("falha de rede do provider: retenta e segue quando a rede volta"; "falha de rede que persiste esgota o teto"), apps/engine/test/engine/dev/dev_agent_server_test.exs ("erro de rede do provider: origem infra e o disjuntor não conta"), apps/engine/test/engine/agents/falha_de_turno_test.exs
    • Origin: AT-484 (TP-01 of 10/10)

    RN-811 — "Already in dev" compares against the remote's updated dev

    • Rule: before the "already in dev" check (RN-797) on report_done, the dev agent updates the remote's dev through the SAME fetch that RN-779 does on claim, serialized per project. Without it the comparison used the dev of the day the worktree was born: the branch of a task whose commits had already entered the remote dev through another task's PR went through QA and SecOps with 0 files. A failing fetch decides nothing — it compares with what there is, and when in doubt the task goes on to the gates.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/worktree_manager.ex:267 (sem_diff_contra_a_dev?), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/worktree_manager.ex:277 (sem_diff_atualizado?), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:892 (handle_outcome)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/tarefa_ja_na_dev_test.exs ("caminho feliz: com a dev atualizada, a branch não tem diff"; "falha: remoto inalcançável não fecha a task sozinha")
    • Origin: AT-486 (TP-01 of 10/10)

    RN-813 — A task that touches the entry point requires evidence that it starts

    • Rule: extension of the cut of RN-765. When the task's title or description touches the entry point, the wiring (routes, migration, registration) or the run README, the QA gate's cut gains the section "Ponto de entrada": require evidence that the REAL entrypoint starts (the project's start/main run, or a test that imports the real entrypoint, not an app assembled in the test itself) and responds on a route or command. Without that evidence, the gap goes to itens. It is a PROMPT yardstick: the gates' external contract (docs/gates.yml) and the emit_qa_verdict code do not change, and no new gate is born. A task that touches none of that does not get the section.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:121 (secao_do_ponto_de_entrada)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa_test.exs ("tarefa que toca a fiação exige evidência do ponto de entrada real" and "tarefa que não toca entrypoint nem fiação não ganha a seção")
    • Origin: AT-483 (TP-01 of 10/10: server.ts never called the migration of the links table and /:code shadowed GET /links; the 53 tests passed because each one assembled its own database and app)

    RN-814 — The QA verdict lists all the gaps in one round

    • Rule: extension of the cut of RN-765: the QA gate lists in itens ALL the task's gaps it finds in the round, at once, never one per round — each round is a paid correction and a step of the cycle's ceiling. The previous prompt only said to list "which rules are missing", and a gap that was not an uncovered rule was left for the next round. Prompt yardstick.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:20 (texto)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa_test.exs ("o recorte pede todas as lacunas numa volta só")
    • Origin: AT-487 (TP-01 of 10/10, task c7ced5a2: two failures for two gaps that already existed in the original delivery)

    RN-817 — QA runs the suite in a clean state, and a skipped test is not green

    • Rule: extension of the cut of RN-765, for every task. Before running the suite, the QA gate shuts down what the project ITSELF left up (the start's server listening on a project port), only processes from that folder, never outside ones. A test that starts a process must terminate the GROUP (or start node directly, not via npm); one that does not terminate hangs the suite in a clean clone and is a gap in itens. A test that SKIPPED itself due to environment (port in use, missing tool) does not count as green: it goes to resumo as an observation. It is an instruction of the cut, not a harness step: the one who runs the suite is QA itself through terminal, which already goes through the policy and the container routing of the three modes — a pkill in the harness would need a second execution path per mode. Prompt yardstick; the gates' contract does not change.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:178 (secao_do_ambiente_limpo)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa_test.exs ("RN-817: pede ambiente limpo, encerrar o grupo e pulado como observação")
    • Origin: AT-496 (TP-01 of 10/10: npm test hung in a clean clone — the test killed npm and the grandchild node stayed on 3000 —, and in the container QA saw exit 0 because the test skipped itself with the port taken by an orphan server)

    RN-818 — The entry point evidence is the one the dev's environment produces

    • Rule: extension of RN-813. The "Ponto de entrada" section says what COUNTS as evidence in the dev's environment — starting the entrypoint's process in the project folder, running the script in the project container, hitting a route of the running process — and what does NOT fit there: building a Docker image, docker compose, external network. A requirement of that second kind never goes to itens; it goes to resumo as an observation. Prompt yardstick.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:121 (secao_do_ponto_de_entrada)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa_test.exs ("RN-818: com ponto de entrada diz o que conta e o que não cabe" and "RN-818: sem ponto de entrada não traz a régua de evidência")
    • Origin: AT-492 (TP-01 of 10/10, task c708f8b2: the 4th round required building the image from the Dockerfile, the project container has no Docker, and the cycle ran out in a politica block)

    RN-819 — QA sweeps rule by rule before emitting

    • Rule: extension of RN-814. Besides listing all the gaps, the cut asks for a SWEEP BY RULE before emitting: go through each business rule and each criterion of the task, one by one, pointing out where the proof is or that it is missing, and only emit after going through all of them. "List all" alone did not prevent a pre-existing gap appearing in the next round. Prompt yardstick.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:20 (texto)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa_test.exs ("RN-819: pede varredura por regra antes de emitir")
    • Origin: AT-490 (TP-01 of 10/10: tasks 2 and 11 brought in the 2nd round a gap that was already in the delivery)

    RN-823 — The gate cycle the engine restart killed is resumed at boot, and the thread says so

    • Rule: at engine BOOT, a RECENT in_progress row of gate_states (below gate_rescue_stale_after_seconds, 15 min, and therefore invisible to the tick) with no live process ON THIS node nor on the OTHER nodes of the cluster is an orphan of the restart, not a slow cycle: the boot resumes it right away (Dispatcher.run_qa/run_secops), without waiting for the threshold. "Alive" is the yardstick of RN-722 (the task in flight in the lead), also asked of the other nodes through :erpc.multicall — a node that does not answer counts as ALIVE, the yardstick of TurnoOrfao (RN-586). The usual guards apply: a pending action of a gate actor does not restart, and an absent dev owner only deletes the row. Before redispatching, the boot records gate.interrupted in the session log (taskId, gate, ultimaAtividadeEm, retomadoEm), and the thread shows it as a notice, in both languages. The periodic tick does NOT change: outside boot, a recent row is a cycle in flight. Declared residue: a node that has not yet joined the cluster at the moment of boot is not asked, and the worst outcome is the usual one (duplicated and cheap work; the second verdict is refused by the api).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/gate_rescuer.ex:191 (run_boot), :209 (interrompido_no_boot), apps/web/src/routes/RetomarGateEstacionado.tsx:33 (cicloInterrompido)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/gate_rescuer_test.exs ("boot com ciclo RECENTE órfão do reinício: grava gate.interrupted e retoma já (RN-823)"; failures: "boot com ciclo recente esperando ação PENDENTE do gate: não reinicia nem grava (RN-823)" and the live local process untouched), apps/web/src/routes/RetomarGateEstacionado.test.tsx ("cicloInterrompido (RN-823)")
    • Origin: AT-495 (TP-01 of 10/10: the 18:51 restart killed the QA cycle of task 10, and the dev stayed awaiting_gate for 15 min without any screen saying so)

    RN-832 — The dev agent knows report_done does commit, push and PR, and that a new test goes into the test script

    • Rule: the dev agent neither has nor needs git_commit, git_push or pr_open: commit, push and PR happen when it calls report_done. The description of report_done, that of report_blocked ("missing git tool" is not a reason to block), the dev agent's kickoff and the environment message (RN-706) say so — the environment one said, until now, to use the typed git actions, which the dev does not have. The kickoff and the environment also say that, if the package.json's test script lists files one by one, every new test file goes into it. Text only: no new code rule, and the Jev stays without a floor for dev agents (RN-758) — until RN-839, which put report_done/report_blocked in their floor.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/tools/report_done.ex:16 (spec), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/tools/report_blocked.ex:12 (spec), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:604 (initial_message), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/ambiente_do_agente.ex:102 (montar)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/report_done_instrucao_test.exs ("caminho feliz: report_done, kickoff e ambiente carregam as duas instruções"; failure: "o ambiente não manda mais usar as ações tipadas de git")
    • Origin: AT-499 (TP-01, 11th: a task blocked with the suite green for "git_commit não existe"; four QA rounds for a test outside the test script)

    RN-845 — The proposed ADRs read says whether the PR has already been merged

    • Rule: on the row of an executed ADR, listar_adrs_propostas (RN-772) no longer asserts "PR aberta" from the ACTION's status: it crosses the result's pullRequestId with the EXECUTED git_merge of the same project (the action every merge goes through, RN-418) and says "PR MERGEADA em <data da decisão>" or "PR aberta (nenhum merge dela registrado no Brabo)". A result without a PR id, or a merge query that fails, becomes "estado da PR: NÃO CONSEGUI LER" — never "aberta". Contained (ADR 0060): one extra query, only over the listed ids, in the same Postgres. Declared limit: a PR closed without a merge OUTSIDE the Brabo (on the remote provider) is not seen, and keeps being read as open — asking the provider would be HTTP in the agent's loop.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tools/listar_adrs_propostas.ex:88 (cruzar_com_merges), :102 (merges_por_pr), :155 (estado_da_pr).
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/tools/listar_adrs_propostas_test.exs ("PR mergeada aparece como mergeada…", "estado da PR ilegível diz que não leu, nunca aberta").
    • Origin: AT-514 (TP-01, 10/11: the Arquiteto said "aberta como PR" 30 s after the merge).

    RN-846 — The HTTP route goes into the README and the input error is the project's

    • Rule: extension of RN-819. The execution dev agent's kickoff asks that an HTTP route created or changed go into the README (method, path, body, responses and codes) and that an input error (malformed JSON, invalid field) respond with its own message in the project's format, never the library's raw message. The QA cut checks both things in the sweep by rule, and whatever is missing goes to itens. Prompt yardstick.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:604 (initial_message) and apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:20 (texto)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/report_done_instrucao_test.exs ("RN-846: kickoff pede a rota no README e erro de entrada próprio") and apps/engine/test/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa_test.exs ("RN-846: confere rota no README e erro de entrada próprio")
    • Origin: AT-515 (TP-01, 12th: the README did not document the API, and malformed JSON responded 400 with the parser's "Expected property name…")

    RN-849 — The architecture readiness turn opens with the current state of the ADRs

    • Rule: extension of RN-845. When the user confirms that the architecture is ready, the Arquiteto's server READS the ADRs' state through the same code as listar_adrs_propostas (local Postgres, no HTTP, same ceiling of 20 lines) and puts it in that turn's opening message, with the instruction not to assert a different ADR state. A failing read says "NÃO CONSEGUI LER" and says to state that it does not know — never "pendente".
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tools/listar_adrs_propostas.ex:50 (contexto_de_prontidao), apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/arquiteto_server.ex:225 (contexto_de_prontidao).
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/tools/listar_adrs_propostas_test.exs ("contexto_de_prontidao/2 (RN-849)").
    • Origin: AT-519 (TP-01: in the readiness turn the Arquiteto said "pendente de aprovação" about an already merged ADR, repeating the previous turn's read).

    RN-850 — The route goes into the "Rotas HTTP" section and the README carries no process note

    • Rule: extension of RN-846. The dev agent's kickoff asks that the route go INSIDE the README's "Rotas HTTP" section (created before "Testes" if missing), never at the end of the file, and that the README, which is the user's, not get a process note nor instructions for agents. The QA cut checks both things. Prompt yardstick.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:604 (initial_message) and apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:20 (texto)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/report_done_instrucao_test.exs ("RN-850: a rota vai dentro de Rotas HTTP e o README não leva nota de processo") and apps/engine/test/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa_test.exs ("RN-850: confere a seção Rotas HTTP e a ausência de nota de processo")
    • Origin: AT-520 (TP-01: each task added routes at the end of the README, after "Testes", and a process paragraph for agents was left over)

    RN-851 — The Infra's compose starts the APP on the user's machine, and the PR declares how to run it

    • Rule: the Infra Lead's kickoff asks for the compose that STARTS THE APP (not only a dev environment): build of each module's Dockerfile with FROM = the decided image (projectImage of the Infra's context; without it, the routing's candidate, stated in the README), port ${APP_PORT:-8080}:<porta interna>, a volume for persistent data, a committed .env.example (never .env) and the section "Rodar na sua máquina" in the README. The agents' WORK container still starts through the broker, and there is no deploy. propose_infra_pr gets the MANDATORY field run (command up to 300 characters; urls from 1 to 5, label up to 60, kind in api_docs | web, url starting with http://localhost: or http://127.0.0.1:; boolean envExample), validated in the engine BEFORE the HALT with a named refusal that enters the loop (RN-163) and passed on in the open_infra_pr payload. Decision in ADR 0213.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/infra/instrucoes_de_execucao.ex:20 (validar), apps/engine/lib/engine/infra/infra_lead_server.ex:1398 (build_kickoff) and :1256 (abrir_pr), and apps/engine/lib/engine/infra/tools/propose_infra_pr.ex:24 (spec)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/infra/infra_lead_server_test.exs (describe "o compose sobe o app e a PR leva run (RN-851)")
    • Origin: AT-517 (the deliverable had no way to be run by the user)

    RN-853 — The dev exposes OpenAPI and Swagger when the task touches routes

    • Rule: part of ADR 0213. When the task touches the entry point or routes (the SAME yardstick as the QA cut, toca_ponto_de_entrada?, never a copy), the dev's kickoff gets the clause: a module that exposes HTTP serves /openapi.json (OpenAPI 3) and /docs (Swagger UI); without network or without the package, /docs is an HTML page that loads swagger-ui from a CDN; a web module serves the home page; the URL goes into the "Rodar na sua máquina" section of the README. A task that does not touch routes does not pay the clause (the message is pinned and comes back on every iteration).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:636 (clausula_de_openapi), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:113 (toca_ponto_de_entrada?)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/report_done_instrucao_test.exs ("RN-853: tarefa que toca rotas pede /openapi.json e /docs"; failure: "RN-853 falha: tarefa sem rota nem entrypoint não paga a cláusula")
    • Origin: AT-517

    RN-854 — QA proves /openapi.json and /docs inside the container

    • Rule: part of ADR 0213. In the entry point section of the cut (RN-813), QA starts the app with the README's command in the background INSIDE the container, hits localhost:<porta interna>/openapi.json and /docs (curl, wget or node -e fetch) and terminates the process group (RN-817). A failure goes to itens; the real docker compose up stays in resumo as "proven only on the user's machine".
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:121 (secao_do_ponto_de_entrada)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa_test.exs ("tarefa que toca rotas pede /openapi.json e /docs, compose em resumo"; failure: "falha: tarefa sem rota não recebe a seção")
    • Origin: AT-517

    RN-862 — The open question on the engine side: tool, verdict and decision in the context

    • Rule: the Criativo, the PO, the Arquiteto and the Dev Lead declare every doubt left for the user with declare_open_question (pergunta up to 500, sugestao up to 300 optional, afeta: {regras, historias, tarefas}), which records artifact.open_question with questionId (uuid), autor and sessionId; an invalid payload is a named refusal and records nothing. The instruction is ONE central sentence, in the flow text of the four (RN-711), never per server. In the QA gate, a requirement "not confirmed by the user" (or contradicted by a current decision) is needs_user_decision, never changes_requested: emit_qa_verdict accepts it only with pergunta or openQuestionId, the QA Lead consolidates it above approved, the verdict goes to the api with the question and the cycle stays PARKED (ADR 0207, gate.rescue_parked, without automatic rescue) until the api asks for resumption through the RN-724 route. The dev-context decisoes enter the dev's kickoff and the QA cut as "Decisões do usuário — vencem RF/DoR quando conflitam" (pergunta → decisao); empty writes nothing. The recorded question counts in the turn's closing (RN-731), and the PO passes question_id in update_story/complete_story when it corrects the story from an answer (ADR 0214).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tools/declare_open_question.ex:54 (run), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/fluxo_do_time.ex:113 (frase_da_pergunta), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/tools/emit_qa_verdict.ex:77 (run), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_lead.ex:89 (consolidar_decisao), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_lead_server.ex:569 (estacionar_por_decisao), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/context_builder.ex:121 (secao_de_decisoes)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/perguntas_em_aberto_test.exs ("válida grava artifact.open_question…"; "inválida é recusa nomeada…"; "consolidação: needs_user_decision vence approved"; "o recorte diz a régua…"; "ContextBuilder: a história leva as decisões…"), apps/engine/test/engine/gates/qa_lead_server_test.exs ("grava a pergunta na api, estaciona o ciclo e só o gesto humano o retoma")
    • Origin: AT-521, ADR 0214

    RN-863 — api_docs is always /docs, every route enters the OpenAPI, and QA fails the missing one

    • Rule: an HTTP module ALWAYS declares /docs in run.urls (the Swagger UI, kind: "api_docs") even before it exists, and a web module the home page — never a business route; the Infra's kickoff says so in the README step (extends RN-851). The run validation refuses, named and with the SAME yardstick on both sides, an api_docs whose URL does not end in /docs (extends RN-852). The dev's clause says that EVERY HTTP route created or changed in the task enters /openapi.json — path with method, body and responses (extends RN-853). In the QA cut, each route of the README's "Rotas HTTP" section must be in paths of the served /openapi.json; a missing route is an item that fails it, never an observation (extends RN-854).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/infra/instrucoes_de_execucao.ex:52 (url), apps/api/src/domain/infra/run-instructions.ts:44 (recusaDoRun), apps/engine/lib/engine/infra/infra_lead_server.ex:1398 (build_kickoff), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:636 (clausula_de_openapi), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:20 (texto)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/infra/infra_lead_server_test.exs ("run api_docs em rota de negócio é recusado com motivo, sem propor"), apps/api/test/application/use-cases/execution/get-run-instructions.use-case.spec.ts ("recusaDoRun (RN-852)"), apps/engine/test/engine/dev/report_done_instrucao_test.exs ("RN-853: …"), apps/engine/test/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa_test.exs ("RN-854: …")
    • Origin: AT-524, ADR 0213

    RN-864 — A transient network failure comes back on its own in up to 3 attempts, and the dev pauses

    • Rule: a task blocked by the dev agent with origin infra for a TRANSIENT network/provider failure — an explicit allowlist: DNS (EAI_AGAIN, ENOTFOUND, getaddrinfo), ECONNRESET, ETIMEDOUT, aborted, the provider's HTTP 5xx/429 (codes rate_limit/upstream, connection/timeout) — is RESUMED on its own up to 3 times, after 1, 3 and 10 min (owner decision), by an Oban job that calls the SAME unblock as the human (UnblockTaskUseCase, through the internal route .../tasks/:taskId/unblock, actor system, a no-op if the task is no longer blocked). The block's reason says which resumption was scheduled; after the 3rd resumption the next failure stays blocked as before, and the reason says the 3 have already been tried. And the agent PAUSES 1 min instead of taking the next task (the wake is ignored during the pause). Exhausted credit (402, RN-726/730) and code errors do NOT enter: they block right away. The resumption counter is only in memory: restarting the engine resets it.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/falha_de_turno.ex:133 (passageira?), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:982 (bloquear_passageira), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/agent_io.ex:293 (pausar_por_rede), apps/engine/lib/engine/workers/retomada_de_tarefa_worker.ex:33 (agendar), apps/api/src/application/use-cases/execution/unblock-task.use-case.ts:33 (ator)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/dev_agent_server_test.exs ("falha passageira: agenda a retomada, pausa e não queima a fila", "fim da pausa volta a reivindicar"; failure: "falha passageira depois de 3 retomadas fica bloqueada pedindo o clique", "402 não agenda retomada"), apps/engine/test/engine/workers/retomada_de_tarefa_worker_test.exs
    • Origin: AT-525

    RN-865 — Endpoint and HTTP verb with a path also switch on the OpenAPI check, and it covers the code's routes

    • Rule: the single yardstick of "the task touches the entry point/routes" (toca_ponto_de_entrada?, used by the QA cut and by the dev's clause of RN-853) also matches "endpoint" and an HTTP verb followed by a path (POST /links). Without it, a task described as "Endpoint protegido…" received neither the OpenAPI clause (dev) nor the /openapi.json check (QA) — that is how POST /links and GET /panel stayed out of the OpenAPI with both tasks approved in TP-01. The QA check now requires in paths of the served /openapi.json, besides the routes of the README's "Rotas HTTP" section, every route the CODE registers; a missing one is an item that fails it (extends RN-863).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:103 (@ponto_de_entrada), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:114 (toca_ponto_de_entrada?)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa_test.exs ("RN-865: tarefa que diz só endpoint ou verbo com caminho recebe a seção"; failure: "falha: tarefa sem rota não recebe a seção")
    • Origin: AT-527

    RN-868 — The gate rescue tick is a single chain

    • Rule: GateRescueSchedulerWorker reschedules its own successor with the SAME uniqueness as kickoff/0 (states available/scheduled/retryable, without executing, a window of two intervals). Before, only kickoff/0 was unique: the job in execution does not count, so the boot's kickoff with the previous tick in flight, or a retry that reruns perform/1, opened a SECOND chain, and they never merged. Measured in real use (10/14): 11 jobs scheduled at the same time and the RN-722 "espera decisão humana" line 11 times per tick for ONE gate_states row. It was not a data defect. With uniqueness on rescheduling the duplicate chains collapse into one on the next tick.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/workers/gate_rescue_scheduler_worker.ex:42 (unicidade)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/workers/gate_rescue_scheduler_worker_test.exs ("o tick se reagenda e mantém a corrente viva"; failure: "com um sucessor já agendado, o tick não abre uma segunda corrente")
    • Origin: AT-529

    RN-874 — The PO receives in the turn the user's decisions and the project's open questions

    • Rule: every PO turn carries, as an EPHEMERAL system message appended by the EngineApiClient facade right after the team flow (RN-711) — never in state.messages nor in the server's prompt —, the user's decisions (artifact.decision_record, choice and context) and the STILL open questions of the project (artifact.open_question without open_question.answered with the same questionId), from ALL sessions. The text says to write the decided ANSWER into the story, rule, DoR, DoD and task, never "pergunta em aberto N"; a still open question is cited by its questionId, without inventing an answer. The read is LOCAL (direct Postgres, without HTTP in the loop), of the 200 most recent events of those types, with a ceiling of 10 decisions, 10 questions, 240 characters per item and 3,000 in the text. Without a decision or an open question, or with the read failing, the turn goes on without the message, never crashes. Only po receives it.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/decisoes_do_projeto.ex:34 (anexar), :49 (texto), apps/engine/lib/engine/session_events/event.ex:224 (list_types_for_project), apps/engine/lib/engine/sessions/engine_api_client.ex:707 (DecisoesDoProjeto)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/decisoes_do_projeto_test.exs ("o turno do PO leva a decisão e só a pergunta AINDA aberta", "custo de entrada tem teto"; failure: "sem decisões nem perguntas não acrescenta nada", "leitura que falha deixa o turno sem a mensagem, sem queda")

    RN-876 — A needs_user_decision refused by the api goes back to QA, and only acceptance parks the gate

    • Rule: the QA gate only PARKS (gate.rescue_parked) when the api accepts the needs_user_decision verdict with nextAction: "await_user". If the api REFUSES (400, 404, or the 409 pergunta_ja_respondida), the refusal goes back to the Automação QA's loop as a new message (RN-163: an error is loop input), resuming the SAME context in which it emitted — on the 409, with the decision already taken for QA to apply instead of asking again. The other delegations do not run again. The return counts toward its own loop's iteration ceiling; once exhausted, the task is blocked with the reason and origin modelo (RN-059), never silently parked. Before, the api's return was discarded and the gate parked without a question in the thread (measured: openQuestionId: "1", a 400 from @IsUUID). The emit_qa_verdict tool refuses, INSIDE the loop and before the api, an openQuestionId that is not a uuid.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_lead_server.ex:575 (devolver_ao_qa), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_lead_server.ex:308 (devolver_ao_qa), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_lead_server.ex:671 (texto_da_recusa), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_automacao_agent.ex:77 (devolver), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/tools/emit_qa_verdict.ex:75 (uuid)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/qa_lead_server_test.exs ("400: nada estaciona, o QA recebe o motivo e uma nova volta acontece"; "409 no laço: a volta seguinte do QA lê a decisão"; failure: "teto esgotado depois da recusa: bloqueio narrado, nunca estacionamento"), apps/engine/test/engine/harness/perguntas_em_aberto_test.exs ("AT-526 (RN-876): openQuestionId que não é uuid é recusa nomeada; uuid passa")
    • Origin: AT-526

    RN-877 — The QA cut says to check the decisions before asking for a decision

    • Rule: the QA cut's instruction says that, before needs_user_decision, it checks the listed decisions; a requirement that one of them already answers is not a question and the decision applies. openQuestionId only with the uuid of an OPEN question; without it, the new question goes in pergunta/sugestao. It is a reading reminder: it does not change the verdict yardstick.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:20 (texto)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa_test.exs ("AT-526 (RN-877): manda conferir as decisões antes de needs_user_decision"; the instruction's failure is covered by the RN-876 test of the tool)
    • Origin: AT-526

    RN-878 — The execution agent's language applies from the first sentence

    • Rule: in the execution agents' turns WITHOUT a human author (dev-*, qa-*), RN-622's language guidance says it applies from the FIRST sentence, including the narration before each tool, and loses the caveat "unless the user asks" (there is no user in the turn). Measured in session 6c68fbc0 (pt-BR project, anthropic/claude-haiku-5.5): the guidance arrived, and even so iteration 0 of qa-automacao and of dev-encurtador-api opened in English ("I'll start by running…"). The path is still ONE (the facade, RN-622): nothing in a server prompt, agent_instructions or tool description (RN-667). The 50-token ceiling (AT-169) holds: measured with gpt-tokenizer, pt-BR 32/26, en 19/19 and the generic one with a short-form code in the worst case 30/30 (cl100k/o200k); a code outside the short form falls back to the usual guidance, which already fit. A turn with an author and the conversational agents do not change. Without a resolved language, nothing is added. Effectiveness is measured in the next TP-01.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/idioma_da_resposta.ex:164 (orientacao_de_execucao), :154 (agente_de_execucao?)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/idioma_da_resposta_test.exs ("agente de execução: vale desde a primeira frase, dentro do teto"; failure: "agente de execução sem idioma resolvido: nada é acrescentado")
    • Origin: AT-530

    RN-879 — The deliverable's absolute URL with the port, and the same code for the same error

    • Rule: when the task touches the entry point or routes, the dev's clause says that an absolute URL returned by the deliverable's API (e.g. the short link) comes from PUBLIC_BASE_URL or from the request's Host WITH the port — never a fixed http://localhost nor the hostname without a port —, and that the same error case responds with the same code on all routes. The QA cut checks both things and treats the divergence (e.g. not_found × link_not_found) as an ITEM. Owner decision of 10/14: a general rule.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:644 (clausula_de_openapi); apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:122 (secao_do_ponto_de_entrada)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/report_done_instrucao_test.exs ("RN-879/880: tarefa que toca rotas leva URL com porta, code único e CORS" and the failure "RN-879/880 falha: tarefa sem rota não recebe CORS nem PUBLIC_BASE_URL"); apps/engine/test/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa_test.exs ("RN-879/880: URL, code de erro e CORS")
    • Origin: AT-531

    RN-880 — The deliverable answers configurable CORS, with relative servers and a pinned swagger-ui

    • Rule: in the same clause, the deliverable's API answers CORS with origins from CORS_ORIGINS (default http://localhost:<porta> and http://127.0.0.1:<porta>), OPTIONS 204 and Access-Control-Allow-* with Authorization and Content-Type; /openapi.json declares servers only relative (/) — an absolute URL would create the CORS error when the page is opened through another host —, and swagger-ui from a CDN goes with an exact version, never @5. The QA proves it with a preflight from another origin (curl -X OPTIONS) and fails it without the headers, with absolute servers or with the floating CDN.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:644 (clausula_de_openapi); apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:122 (secao_do_ponto_de_entrada)
    • Test: the same as RN-879 (happy path and failure in both files)
    • Origin: AT-532

    RN-881 — The engine prints the project's other decisions for the dev and QA

    • Rule: the decisoesDoProjeto the api returns in dev-context (RN-872, ADR 0215, already with a ceiling) becomes the section "Outras decisões do projeto", separate and AFTER the task's decisions, in the dev's story unit and in the QA cut. Empty or absent writes nothing; the engine applies no ceiling of its own.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/context_builder.ex:133 (secao_de_decisoes_do_projeto); apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:20 (texto)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/context_builder_test.exs ("RN-881: decisoesDoProjeto vira a seção..." and "RN-881 falha: sem decisoesDoProjeto a seção some"); apps/engine/test/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa_test.exs ("RN-881: outras decisões do projeto")
    • Origin: AT-526 (slice F4, engine side)

    RN-885 — The deliverable's CORS uses the PUBLISHED port, and QA checks the header on both origins

    • Rule: revises RN-880 (which still holds for the rest). The default of the deliverable's CORS_ORIGINS uses the PUBLISHED port — APP_PORT, which the compose passes to the app —, never the internal PORT: in TP-01 (16th) the default came out with PORT=3000 and, with the compose publishing 8080, no real origin received Access-Control-Allow-Origin (measured with curl: :3000 receives, :8080 does not; in Chrome, Failed to fetch). QA runs the preflight against the PUBLISHED port, once for http://localhost:<APP_PORT> and again for http://127.0.0.1:<APP_PORT>, and fails it without the Access-Control-Allow-Origin header — a 204 alone is not enough, and CORS only on the internal port is an ITEM.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:644 (clausula_de_openapi); apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:122 (secao_do_ponto_de_entrada)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/report_done_instrucao_test.exs ("RN-885: o padrão do CORS usa a porta PUBLICADA, nunca a interna"; failure: "RN-879/880 falha: tarefa sem rota não recebe CORS nem PUBLIC_BASE_URL"); apps/engine/test/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa_test.exs ("RN-885: o preflight é na porta PUBLICADA, nas duas origens, e confere o header")
    • Origin: AT-535

    RN-890 — QA demands the command-line exit code, on refusal too

    • Rule: the Automação QA's instruction says to check, in the test that proves a command-line command of the delivery (CLI, package.json script), the exit code 0 on the happy path and non-zero when the command refuses the input; a test without that check on refusal is a rule without coverage and goes to itens. The QA-strategy instruction asks, in criteriosExecutaveis, for these two criteria for every command that refuses input. It is an agent instruction: no new gate, no change of verdict or structure.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_automacao_agent.ex:142 (instrucao_de_codigo_de_saida), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_estrategia_agent.ex:129 (instrucao_de_codigo_de_saida)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/qa_automacao_agent_test.exs ("a instrução cobra código de saída 0 no feliz e diferente de 0 na recusa"); apps/engine/test/engine/gates/qa_estrategia_agent_test.exs ("os arquivos da entrega entram na mensagem")
    • Origin: AT-542 (TP-01, 17th: npm run user:create refused the password and exited with 0)

    RN-893 — The PO declares the edge of the provided value in the RF and the DoD

    • Rule: the PO's kickoff instruction (with or without an event log) carries a third obligation: every RF that receives a value provided by the user (date, number, text, list, limit) declares, in the RF and the DoD, whether the edge — past/future, zero, empty, negative, the limit itself — is ACCEPTED or REFUSED (with the HTTP status/error); the example is an expiration in the past. When unsure, the PO calls declare_open_question with afeta.historias and a suggestion, never inventing or omitting. A PROMPT rule, in the mold of RN-790: no tool refuses a story without an edge. The PO does NOT start rewriting business_rule; Creative, QA, schema and gate do not change.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/po_server.ex:649 (obrigacoes)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/agents/po_server_test.exs:438 (the kickoff instruction carries the edge obligation) and :460 (without an event log, the kickoff still carries it)
    • Origin: AT-541 (TP-01 of 10/14: the deliverable accepted expiresAt in the past and the link was born expired, 410; the story's RF only said the expiration is optional), owner's decision of 10/15

    RN-895 — The argument refusal says what arrived and the expected format

    • Rule: revises RN-719 and RN-725. A field declared array/object that arrives as non-JSON text, or as a number/boolean/wrong type, is refused before the tool with "the field X arrived as ; send ", with an example (["item 1","item 2"], {"chave":"valor"}), never with the value. The emit_plano_de_teste sentence now puts the keys in backticks, so the refusal carries the received keys.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/argumentos_de_ferramenta.ex:80 (coagir); apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/tools/emit_plano_de_teste.ex:73 (run)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/argumentos_de_ferramenta_test.exs:193 and :201 (the format and the received type) and :210 (the received keys)
    • Origin: AT-583 (TP-01 18th and 19th: dod arrived as a markdown checklist and the test plan was refused 4 times without saying what came)

    RN-896 — The epic_id is validated before the api

    • Rule: create_story refuses an epic_id that is not a UUID ("epic_id inválido; …"). When the PO already created epics in this process (epicos_conhecidos) and the id is not among them, it rereads the backlog ONCE (list_backlog): if the id is there, it accepts; otherwise it refuses listing the backlog's id=title (cap of 20 lines, stating the total if truncated, ADR 0060). An empty map or a failing backlog read means "unknown" and does not refuse — the api decides.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tools/create_story.ex:57 (epico_valido)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/argumentos_de_ferramenta_test.exs:224 and :233
    • Origin: AT-584 (qwen sent EPIC_ID_PLACEHOLDER_NAO_USAR_AQUI and the api answered with the DTO name)

    RN-897 — A provider cut by max_tokens in a gate is origin modelo

    • Rule: the agent.error of the qa-estrategia, qa-automacao, qa-performance-seguranca and appsec gates classifies "Tool calls cutoff by max_tokens" as modelo (the reasoning ate the cap, RN-059); any other LLM error stays infra.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/origem_do_erro.ex:10 (de_llm)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/origem_do_erro_test.exs:7
    • Origin: AT-586 (BaseTen 502 recorded as infra)

    RN-898 — A response cut by the cap with no text closes the turn, stated

    • Rule: a conversational agent turn whose last LLM response was cut by the output cap (RN-737) and wrote no text in any round does NOT close silently: the line "Response cut off by the length limit." becomes the closing agent.response with modelName, and the bubble shows "Continue where it left off" (RN-749). It covers the seven conversational agents.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/texto_do_turno.ex:65 (payload_do_turno)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/resposta_cortada_test.exs:104
    • Origin: AT-570 (TP-01 18th)

    RN-899 — An empty response without a cut becomes an agent.error of origin model

    • Rule: a conversational loop closing with no text, no tool and no cut records agent.error with origin modelo and "the model returned no response" (FalhaDeTurno), in the seven servers. It is an agent utterance for the heartbeat (RN-581); per-tab project presence does NOT enter (RN-064).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/falha_de_turno.ex:63 (origem), apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/po_server.ex:303 (gravar_texto_do_turno)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/agents/po_server_test.exs:194 and apps/engine/test/engine/agents/texto_do_turno_test.exs:96
    • Origin: AT-572 (TP-01 18th, finding 4)

    RN-900 — write_file refuses to rewrite the README and drop the Infra's usage section

    • Rule: write_file replaces the whole file. When the path is a README (README*), the current file has the ## Rodar na sua máquina section and the new content does not, the tool refuses with a named error and writes nothing. Changing a fragment requires rewriting the full README, keeping the section. The title is one constant (titulo_secao_de_uso/0), also used by the Infra and dev prompts, so the refusal and the instruction do not diverge. It is not a gate: the refusal belongs to the tool itself and does not cover writes by terminal (cat >, sed -i).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tools/write_file.ex:62 (recusa_de_secao_perdida)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/tools/write_file_test.exs:41 (a rewrite without the section is refused and the file stays intact); the happy path is covered by the first two tests in the same file
    • Origin: AT-571 (TP-01 18th run of 10/19: the dev agent rewrote the README while fixing a QA finding and the usage section the Infra had written was lost; no gate noticed)

    RN-903 — The provider's 429 is the model's usage limit, not a network failure

    • Rule: a task the dev agent blocks because the model answered 429 (code rate_limit, or text with (429) or "limite de uso") says in the reason "limite de uso do modelo atingido (429)", and the automatic retry says that, if it repeats, the dev agent's model should be changed. It never says "falha passageira de rede" (transient network failure). The origin stays infra and the RN-864 retry does not change (up to 3, after 1, 3 and 10 min; a 60 s pause, which dev.idle names as "depois de limite de uso do modelo"). A network failure keeps its usual text.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:1136 (limite_de_uso?), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:1127 (motivo_da_parada), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:997 (bloquear_passageira), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/agent_io.ex:293 (pausar_por_rede)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/dev_agent_server_test.exs ("429 do provider: o motivo diz limite de uso do modelo, não rede"; failure: "falha de rede continua com o texto de rede, não de limite")
    • Origin: AT-594 (TP-01 20th, 10/19: the 429 of laguna-s-2.1:free blocked the tasks as "falha passageira de rede", while the diagnosis said usage limit)

    RN-904 — The output-ceiling cut is not repeated with the full catalog

    • Rule: when Jev restricts the menu and the model answers WITHOUT a tool, the step is repeated ONCE with the full catalog (RN-625) — except when the response was CUT by the output ceiling (truncated). The cut is not a menu error: retrying with the full catalog would only enlarge the prompt and spend another output ceiling. It applies to both facades (llm_turn and llm_turn_stream, the latter only without text already delivered). The RN-780 retry in ToolLoop handles the cut and does not change.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/roteamento_de_ferramenta.ex:126 (repetir_com_catalogo_inteiro?)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/roteamento_de_ferramenta_test.exs:274 (an empty cut response is not repeated, in both paths; without a cut it is)
    • Origin: AT-578 (EP-026, survey of 10/21): the PO's first turn in the 18th round spent two 8192-token output rounds.

    RN-905 — The step ceiling says what was recorded and what is missing, and offers to continue

    • Rule: when a conversational agent stops at the step limit, the ceiling event (toolloop.limit_reached; for the Creative, the agent.error with reason: "limite_de_iteracoes") carries gravado, the RN-731 line read BEFORE the turn text is written (it goes there and not in the agent.response, never in both). The PO also carries regrasSemHistoria, the uncoveredCount that fechar_handoff already reads; a failed read leaves the key absent, never an error. The thread shows the reason without jargon, the recorded line, the rules without a story and the "Continue where you left off" button (RN-749, a user message that the RN-673 queue absorbs while a turn runs) only for the seven conversational agents with the session active; dev-*/qa-* get the text, with no button. The button never continues on its own.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/po_server.ex:257 (run_turn); apps/web/src/routes/session-timeline-montagem.tsx:976 (conversacional)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/agents/po_server_test.exs:548 (gravado and regrasSemHistoria; without the read, no key); apps/web/src/routes/session-timeline-montagem.falha-de-turno.test.tsx:108 (bubble, button to the same agent; no button for dev-* and inactive session)
    • Origin: AT-590 (EP-031).

    RN-908 — The PO kickoff asks for the backlog in batches, after reading, by layer

    • Rule: the PO kickoff tells the model, in this order: read listar_regras_de_negocio and listar_backlog in the same response, with no write alongside; then all epics in one response; then all stories, using the epic_id returned; then all tasks, using the story_id returned. Never recreate an epic that already exists, and at most about 10 tool calls per response. Measured reason: without the batch request, nemotron makes 1 tool per response and qwen makes 3; with it, 9 each (parallel_tool_calls changed nothing). Reading first is what stops qwen from creating epics without reading the backlog. It does not change the @max_iterations ceiling (RN-166).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/po_server.ex:614 (build_kickoff)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/agents/po_server_test.exs:518 (read before write; batch by layer: epics → stories → tasks)
    • Origin: probe of AT-591 (21/10), implemented in AT-596 (EP-026).
